PHASE 5 V9 RECOVERY INTERPRETATION
================================================================================

This report summarizes recovery after corrected Phase 4 detection.

Phase 5 recovery rule:
  if W10_N3_mean1x detection is active:
      recovered_future = V9 trained hybrid software-sensor prediction
  else:
      recovered_future = attacked future measurement

Important:
  - Phase 4 detection was not redone.
  - Phase 5 only consumes corrected Phase 4 detection CSVs.
  - Roll/Pitch error is normal subtraction.
  - Yaw error is circular angular difference.
